      SQL structure -- a column expression, a table name -- has to be interpolated, because it cannot be a bound parameter. A VALUE can be, and must be. An interpolated expression passes here only if it is one of:
        1. a call to ['_validate_table_name', 'json_text'], which raise on anything but an identifier
        2. a name assigned from one of those calls
        3. a loop variable over a literal sequence of strings
        4. an UPPER_CASE name assigned a string literal
      If the value is request data, bind it as a parameter. If it is structure, route it through a validator that RAISES -- not one that sanitises.
